SELECT [c].[Id], [c].[ConversationId], [c].[CreatedAt], [c].[IsArchived], [c].[IsDeleted], [c].[LastReadAt], [c].[UnreadCount], [c].[UpdatedAt], [c].[UserId], [c0].[Id], [c0].[CreatedAt], [c0].[IsDeleted], [c0].[LastMessageAt], [c0].[LastMessagePreview], [c0].[UpdatedAt], [s].[Id], [s].[ConversationId], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[IsArchived], [s].[IsDeleted], [s].[LastReadAt], [s].[UnreadCount], [s].[UpdatedAt], [s].[UserId], [s].[Id0], [s].[AccessFailedCount], [s].[AuthProvider], [s].[Bio], [s].[CodeExpireDate], [s].[ConcurrencyStamp], [s].[CreatedAt0], [s].[DistanceUnit], [s].[Email], [s].[EmailConfirmed], [s].[FullName], [s].[GoogleId], [s].[GymId], [s].[Language], [s].[LockoutEnabled], [s].[LockoutEnd], [s].[NormalizedEmail], [s].[NormalizedUserName], [s].[NotifyEmail], [s].[NotifyNewFeatures], [s].[NotifyPromotions], [s].[NotifyPush], [s].[NotifySessionUpdates], [s].[NotifySms], [s].[NotifyWorkoutReminders], [s].[PasswordHash], [s].[PhoneNumber], [s].[PhoneNumberConfirmed], [s].[ProfileImagePath], [s].[ProfileImageUrl], [s].[SecurityStamp], [s].[Theme], [s].[TimeZone], [s].[TwoFactorEnabled], [s].[UserName], [s].[VerificationCode]
FROM [ConversationParticipants] AS [c]
INNER JOIN [Conversations] AS [c0] ON [c].[ConversationId] = [c0].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [c1].[Id], [c1].[ConversationId], [c1].[CreatedAt], [c1].[IsArchived], [c1].[IsDeleted], [c1].[LastReadAt], [c1].[UnreadCount], [c1].[UpdatedAt], [c1].[UserId], [a].[Id] AS [Id0], [a].[AccessFailedCount], [a].[AuthProvider], [a].[Bio], [a].[CodeExpireDate], [a].[ConcurrencyStamp], [a].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt0], [a].[DistanceUnit], [a].[Email], [a].[EmailConfirmed], [a].[FullName], [a].[GoogleId], [a].[GymId], [a].[Language], [a].[LockoutEnabled], [a].[LockoutEnd], [a].[NormalizedEmail], [a].[NormalizedUserName], [a].[NotifyEmail], [a].[NotifyNewFeatures], [a].[NotifyPromotions], [a].[NotifyPush], [a].[NotifySessionUpdates], [a].[NotifySms], [a].[NotifyWorkoutReminders], [a].[PasswordHash], [a].[PhoneNumber], [a].[PhoneNumberConfirmed], [a].[ProfileImagePath], [a].[ProfileImageUrl], [a].[SecurityStamp], [a].[Theme], [a].[TimeZone], [a].[TwoFactorEnabled], [a].[UserName], [a].[VerificationCode]
    FROM [ConversationParticipants] AS [c1]
    INNER JOIN [AspNetUsers] AS [a] ON [c1].[UserId] = [a].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [c0].[Id] = [s].[ConversationId]
WHERE [c].[UserId] = @currentUserId AND [c].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit) AND [c].[IsArchived] = CAST(0 AS bit)
ORDER BY COALESCE([c0].[LastMessageAt], [c0].[CreatedAt]) DESC, [c].[Id], [c0].[Id], [s].[Id]
